公司簡介
COMPANY PROFILE
華潤萬象生活有限公司（以下簡稱：華潤萬象生      China Resources Mixc Lifestyle Services Limited (“CR Mixc
活，股票代碼1209.HK）是中國領先的物業管理   Lifestyle”) (stock code: 1209. HK) is the leading property
及商業運營服務商。華潤萬象生活堅持圍繞「成      management and commercial operational services provider
為客戶信賴和喜愛的城市品質生活服務商」的企      in China. Upholding to our corporate vision to “become a
業願景，憑藉卓越的運營服務能力、豐富的生態      trustworthy and popular urban quality-life service provider
資源及強大的一體化會員體系，致力打造「全業      among customers”, CR Mixc Lifestyle fully capitalizes
態、全客戶、全產品與全服務」的城市品質生活      on the superior operational capabilities, abundant
服務平台。商管業務聚焦高端與品質購物中心賽      ecological resources and strong integrated membership
道，以「萬象城」、「萬象匯」、「萬象天地」為核    system to build an urban quality-life service platform
心品牌，憑藉行業領先的商業運營能力，堅持高      featured with “multiple property types, all customers
品質規模發展，持續打造「中國商業運營管理第      and full spectrum of product and service offerings”. Our
一品牌」。物管業務聚焦品質社區、城市公共空      commercial management business focuses on high-end
間、寫字樓及產業園三大賽道，以「萬象服務」      and high-quality shopping mall segment and, by promoting
為核心品牌，堅持全域、全業態佈局，致力成為      “MIXC”, “MIXC ONE” and “MIXC World” as our core brands
「中國最具影響力的城市空間運營服務商」。截至     and leveraging on our industrial-leading commercial
2022年12月31日，華潤萬象生活提供物業管理   operational capabilities, our aim of high quality and
服務的住宅及商業物業建築面積已達約299.2 百   large-scale development has been persistently pursued,
万平方米，提供商業運營服務的購物中心及寫字      striving to become “the PRC’s No.1 brand in commercial
樓建築面積約11.0百萬平方米。未來，公司將加    operational and management”. For property management
大對科技能力的投入，將科技創新作為公司最核      business, concentrating on three main segments, namely
心的優勢，持續打造一體化生態體系，助力城市      high-quality residential projects, urban public space and
發展，引領美好生活。                 office buildings and industrial parks, and using “MIXC
                           Services” as the core brand, we uphold to our business
                           layout of covering all property types across all regions
                           so as to become “the PRC’s most influential urban space
                           operational service provider”. As of 31 December 2022,
                           the GFA of the residential and commercial properties under
                           our property management services was approximately
                           299.2 million sq.m. and the GFA of the shopping malls
                           and office buildings under our commercial operational
                           services was approximately 11.0 million sq.m. In the
                           future, the Company will further increase investment in
                           technology and take technological innovation as the core
                           edge to continuously strengthen our integrated ecosystem,
                           facilitate urban development and play a leading role in
                           creating people’s happy life.

        主席報告
        CHAIRMAN’
                S STATEMENT
        本人欣然向各位股東提呈本集團二零二二年十二                                   I am pleased to present the annual business review for the
        月三十一日止之年度（「本年度」）業績回顧與展                                  year ended 31 December 2022 (“Year”) and outlook of the
        望。                                                      Group to the shareholders.


        二零二二年，國際局勢波譎雲詭，加上其他因                                    In 2022, as the international situation was turbulent and
        素，中國經濟增長遭遇嚴峻挑戰。中國政府及                                    also due to other factors, China encountered severe
        時出台「穩經濟」一攬子政策，有效應對多重衝                                   challenges in economic growth. The Chinese government
        擊，確保宏觀經濟大盤運行總體穩定。全年國內                                   issued a package of policies to “stabilize the economy” in
        生產總值首次突破人民幣120萬億元關口，實際                                  a timely manner to effectively cope with multiple shocks
        增長領跑全球主要經濟體，社會消費品零售總                                    and ensure the overall stability of the macro economy.
        額保持穩定，鞏固全球第二大消費市場地位，展                                   The annual gross domestic product of China exceeded
        現出中國的強大韌性與增長潛力。房地產市場持                                   RMB120 trillion for the first time, leading global major
        續出清，頭部房企市場佔有率穩步提升，行業加                                   economies in terms of real growth. The total retail sales

外匯風險                            FOREIGN CURRENCY RISK
由於本集團業務主要於中國進行，本集團主要採           As the Group’s business is mainly conducted in the PRC,
用人民幣作為結算貨幣。截至二零二二年十二月           we mainly adopt RMB as the settlement currency. As of
三十一日止，非人民幣資產及負債主要為現金港           31 December 2022, non RMB assets and liabilities mainly
幣35.75 百萬元及83,301.23 美元。本集團已於   included the cash of HK$35.75 million and US$83,301.23,
二零二二年七月二十九日全部結清港幣借款，管           The Group has settled in full the HK dollar denominated
理層認為本集團在經營方面並無重大外匯風險，           borrowings on 29 July 2022. The management believed
人民幣匯率波動不會對本集團財務狀況帶來顯著           that the operation of the Group was not exposed to
影響。本集團目前並無外幣風險對沖政策，然            material foreign currency risk. No significant impact was
而，管理層將對外匯風險敞口實施動態監控並將           caused by the fluctuation of RMB exchange rate on the
根據市場環境的變化進行必要調整。                Group’s financial position. Currently, the Group does not
                                have any hedging policies against its foreign exchange
                                risk, but the management will actively monitor the foreign
                                exchange exposure and make necessary adjustments in
                                accordance with the changes in market environment.

                         Diversity Policy”.

以及本公司採納的董事會成員多元化政策。董事    Director is a collective decision of the Board, taking into
會認為，董事會組成的變動不應對本公司帶來過    consideration the procedures for Shareholders to propose
度干擾，並繼續確保執行董事與非執行董事（包    a person for election as a Director of the Company and the

提供在任何財務年度不少於可供分配之年度核心    shareholders of the Company with an annual dividend

限於股息宣派及派付的限制）。           and payment of dividends shall remain to be determined
                         at the absolute discretion of the Board and subject to

截至二零二二年十二月三十一日止年度，我們大        For the year ended 31 December 2022, a significant
部分物業管理及商業運營服務與華潤集團及華潤        portion of our property management and commercial
置地開發的物業有關。截至二零二二年十二月三        operational services was related to properties developed
十一日止年度，來自本集團五大客戶的收入額佔        by CR Group and CR Land. For the year ended 31
本集團總收入的33.2%（二零二一年：41.3%），   December 2022, the revenue amounts from the Group’s
而來自本集團單一最大客戶華潤置地的收入額佔        five largest customers accounted for 33.2% (2021: 41.3%)
本集團總收入的28.0%（二零二一年：35.3%）。   of the Group’s total revenue and the revenue amount from

意見                                      OPINION
本核數師（以下簡稱「我們」）已審核列載於第147                We have audited the consolidated financial statements
頁至第295頁華潤萬象生活有限公司（「貴公司」）                of China Resources Mixc Lifestyle Services Limited (the
及其附屬公司（以下統稱「貴集團」）的綜合財務                  “Company”) and its subsidiaries (the “Group”) set out
報表，此綜合財務報表包括於二零二二年十二月                   on pages 147 to 295, which comprise the consolidated
三十一日的綜合財務狀況表，以及截至該日止年                   statement of financial position as at 31 December 2022,

         核數師就審核綜合財務報表須承擔                                         A U D I T O R ’S R E S P O N S I B I L I T I E S F O R
         的責任                                                     THE AUDIT OF THE CONSOLIDATED
                                                                 FINANCIAL STATEMENTS
         我們的目標乃對綜合財務報表整體是否不存在由                                   Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about
         於欺詐或錯誤而導致的重大錯誤陳述取得合理保                                   whether the consolidated financial statements as a whole are
         證，並出具包括我們意見的核數師報告。我們的                                   free from material misstatement, whether due to fraud or error,
         報告僅向 閣下（作為整體）呈報，除此之外別                                   and to issue an auditor’s report that includes our opinion.
         無其他目的。我們概不就本報告的內容對任何其                                   Our report is made solely to you, as a body, and for no other
         他人士負責或承擔責任。                                             purpose. We do not assume responsibility towards or accept
                                                                 liability to any other person for the contents of this report.

         合理保證為高水平的保證，但不能保證按照香港                                   Reasonable assurance is a high level of assurance, but
         審計準則進行的審核總能發現所存在的重大錯誤                                   is not a guarantee that an audit conducted in accordance
         陳述。錯誤陳述可以由欺詐或錯誤引起，如果合                                   with HKSAs will always detect a material misstatement
         理預期其單獨或匯總起來可能影響使用者依賴綜                                   when it exists. Misstatements can arise from fraud or
         合財務報表所作出的經濟決定，則有關的錯誤陳                                   error and are considered material if, individually or in the
         述可被視作重大。                                                aggregate, they could reasonably be expected to influence
                                                                 the economic decisions of users taken on the basis of
                                                                 these consolidated financial statements.

2.4 關鍵會計政策概要            2.4 S U M M A R Y O F S I G N I F I C A N T
                            ACCOUNTING POLICIES
  本集團於編製本報告所載財務報表時採納        The principal accounting policies adopted by the
  的主要會計政策載列如下：              Group in arriving at these financial statements set
                            out in this report are set out below:


  於聯營公司和合營公司的投資             Investments in associates and joint
                            ventures
  聯營公司指本集團持有其通常不少於20%       An associate is an entity in which the Group has a
  股本表決權的長期權益並可對其發揮重大        long term interest of generally not less than 20%
  影響力的實體。重大影響力為可參與投資        of the equity voting rights and over which it is in a
  對象的財務及營運政策決定，而非控制或        position to exercise significant influence. Significant
  共同控制該等政策。                 influence is the power to participate in the financial
                            and operating policy decisions of the investee, but
                            is not control or joint control over those policies.


  合營企業是指合營方對該安排的淨資產享        A joint venture is a type of joint arrangement
  有權利的合營安排。共同控制是指按照相        whereby the parties that have joint control of the
  關約定對某項安排所共有的控制，並且該        arrangement have rights to the net assets of the joint

  與方一致同意後才能決策。              sharing of control of an arrangement, which exists
                            only when decisions about the relevant activities
                            require the unanimous consent of the parties sharing
                            control.

                公允價值計量                                               Fair value measurement
                本集團於各報告期末按公允價值計量其投                                   The Group measures its investment properties and
                資物業及以公允價值計量且其變動計入當                                   financial assets at fair value through profit or loss
                期收益的金融資產。公允價值指於計量日                                   at fair value at the end of each reporting period.
                期市場參與者之間的有序交易中，就出售                                   Fair value is the price that would be received to sell
                資產所收取的價格或轉讓負債所支付的價                                   an asset or paid to transfer a liability in an orderly
                格。公允價值計量乃基於假設出售資產或                                   transaction between market participants at the
                轉讓負債的交易於資產或負債的主要市                                    measurement date. The fair value measurement is
                場，或在未有主要市場的情況下，則於資                                   based on the presumption that the transaction to
                產或負債最有利市場進行。主要或最有利                                   sell the asset or transfer the liability takes place
                市場須位於本集團能到達的地方。資產或                                   either in the principal market for the asset or liability,
                負債的公允價值乃使用市場參與者為資產                                   or in the absence of a principal market, in the most
                或負債定價所用的假設計量（假設市場參                                   advantageous market for the asset or liability. The
                與者依照彼等最佳經濟利益行事）。                                     principal or the most advantageous market must be
                                                                     accessible by the Group. The fair value of an asset
                                                                     or a liability is measured using the assumptions
                                                                     that market participants would use when pricing the
                                                                     asset or liability, assuming that market participants
                                                                     act in their economic best interest.


                非金融資產公允價值之計量則參考市場參                                   A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset
                與者可從使用該資產得到之最高及最佳效                                   takes into account a market participant’s ability to
                用，或把該資產售予另一可從使用該資產                                   generate economic benefits by using the asset in
                得到最高及最佳效用之市場參與者所產生                                   its highest and best use or by selling it to another
                之經濟效益。                                               market participant that would use the asset in its
                                                                     highest and best use.

        最低層級的輸入數據是無法                             unobservable

  預期信貸虧損分兩個階段確認。就自初始         ECLs are recognised in two stages. For credit
  確認起信貸風險並未大幅增加的信貸風險         exposures for which there has not been a significant
  而言，預期信貸虧損就可能於未來 12 個       increase in credit risk since initial recognition, ECLs
  月內發生的違約事件導致的信貸虧損作出         are provided for credit losses that result from default
  撥備（12 個月預期信貸虧損）。就自初始       events that are possible within the next 12 months (a
  確認起信貸風險已大幅增加的信貸風險而         12-month ECL). For those credit exposures for which
  言，須於風險的餘下年期內就信貸虧損作         there has been a significant increase in credit risk
  出虧損撥備（不論違約時間） （整個存續期       since initial recognition, a loss allowance is required
  預期信貸虧損）。                   for credit losses expected over the remaining life
                             of the exposure, irrespective of the timing of the

  於各報告日期，本集團評估金融工具的信         At each reporting date, the Group assesses
  貸風險自初始確認起是否已大幅增加。在         whether the credit risk on a financial instrument
  作出評估時，本集團將金融工具於報告日         has increased significantly since initial recognition.
  期發生違約的風險與金融工具於初始確認         When making the assessment, the Group compares
  日期發生違約的風險進行比較，考慮無需         the risk of a default occurring on the financial
  過多成本或努力即可獲得的合理有支持資         instrument as at the reporting date with the risk
  料（包括歷史及前瞻性資料）。本集團認         of a default occurring on the financial instrument

  風險顯著增加。                    reasonable and supportable information that is
                             available without undue cost or effort, including
                             historical and forward-looking information. The
                             Group considers that there has been a significant
                             increase in credit risk when contractual payments
                             are more than 30 days past due.

                當合同付款逾期 90 天時，本集團認為金                                 The Group considers a financial asset in default
                融資產已違約。然而，在若干情況下，當                                   when contractual payments are 90 days past due.
                內部或外部資料表明本集團不大可能收回                                   However, in certain cases, the Group may also
                全部未收回的合同款項（在考慮本集團持                                   consider a financial asset to be in default when
                有的任何信貸增強措施前）時，本集團亦                                   internal or external information indicates that
                可能將金融資產視為違約。當合理預期無                                   the Group is unlikely to receive the outstanding
                法收回合同現金流量時，金融資產予以撇                                   contractual amounts in full before taking into
                銷。                                                   account any credit enhancements held by the
                                                                     Group. A financial asset is written off when there
                                                                     is no reasonable expectation of recovering the
                                                                     contractual cash flows.

  簡易法                      Simplified approach

  就不包含重大融資部分或本集團應用不調       For trade and bill receivables that do not contain a
  整重大融資部分的影響的簡化方法的貿易       significant financing component or when the Group
  應收款項及應收票據而言，本集團在計算       applies the practical expedient of not adjusting
  預期信貸虧損時應用簡易法。根據簡易        the effect of a significant financing component, the
  法，本集團不跟蹤信貸風險的變化，而是       Group applies the simplified approach in calculating
  於各報告日期根據整個存續期預期信貸虧       ECLs. Under the simplified approach, the Group
  損確認虧損撥備。本集團已基於其過往信       does not track changes in credit risk, but instead
  貸虧損經驗建立撥備矩陣，並就債務及經       recognises a loss allowance based on lifetime ECLs
  濟環境的特定前瞻性因素作出調整。         at each reporting date. The Group has established
                           a provision matrix that is based on its historical
                           credit loss experience, adjusted for forward-looking
                           factors specific to the debtors and the economic
                           environment.

  的交易成本。                   payables, net of directly attributable transaction

                                         item, it is recognised as income on a systematic
                                         basis over the periods that the costs, for which it is
                                         intended to compensate, are expensed.


  如補助涉及資產，則公允價值計入遞延收                     Where the grant relates to an asset, the fair value
  入賬戶，並按等額年度分期在相關資產的                     is credited to a deferred income account and is
  預計可使用年期撥回綜合損益及其他全面                     released to the consolidated statement of profit
  收益表，或透過降低折舊費用而從資產的                     or loss and other comprehensive income over
  賬面值中扣除並撥回綜合損益及其他全面                     the expected useful life of the relevant asset by
  收益表。                                   equal annual instalments or deducted from the
                                         carrying amount of the asset and released to
                                         the consolidated statement of profit or loss and
                                         other comprehensive income by way of a reduced
                                         depreciation charge.

     租賃 － 估計增量借款利率             Leases – Estimating the incremental borrowing rate

     本集團無法輕易釐定租賃中隱含的利率，        The Group cannot readily determine the interest
     因此，本集團使用增量借款利率計量租賃        rate implicit in a lease, and therefore, it uses an
     負債。增量借款利率指本集團在類似經濟        incremental borrowing rate (“IBR”) to measure
     環境下按類似期限、以類似抵押品借入取        lease liabilities. The IBR is the rate of interest that
     得價值與使用權資產接近的資產所需的資        the Group would have to pay to borrow over a
     金而須支付的利率。因此，增量借款利率        similar term, and with a similar security, the funds
     反映本集團「須支付」的利率，當並無可        necessary to obtain an asset of a similar value
     觀察利率時（如對並無訂立融資交易的附        to the right-of-use asset in a similar economic
     屬公司而言），或需要調整利率以反映租        environment. The IBR therefore reflects what
     賃條款及條件時（例如，當租賃並非以附        the Group “would have to pay”, which requires
     屬公司的功能貨幣訂立時），需要估計該        estimation when no observable rates are available
     利率。當可觀察輸入數據（如市場利率）        (such as for subsidiaries that do not enter into
     可用時，本集團使用可觀察輸入數據估算        financing transactions) or when it needs to be
     遞增借貸利率並須作出若干實體特定的估        adjusted to reflect the terms and conditions of
     計（如附屬公司單獨的信貸評級）。          the lease. (for example, when leases are not in
                               the subsidiary’s functional currency). The Group
                               estimates the IBR using observable inputs (such as
                               market interest rates) when available and is required
                               to make certain entity-specific estimates (such as
                               the subsidiary’s stand-alone credit rating).

17. 定期存款                    17. TIME DEPOSITS
   餘額指從中國內地信譽卓著的持牌銀行購          The balance represents deposit certificates
   買的大額存單，固定年利率為3.5%，原         purchased from a creditworthy licensed bank in
   訂期限為 36 個月。大額存單於持有超過        Chinese Mainland earning interest at a fixed rate of
   三個月時可予以贖回。定期存款的合同條          3.5% per annum with an original maturity period of
   款於特定日期產生的現金流量僅為對本金          36 months. The deposits certificates are redeemable
   及未償還本金利息的付款，並按目標為持          upon holding for longer than three months. The
   有及獲取合同現金流量的業務模式持有。          contractual terms of the time deposits give rise
   因此，定期存款入賬列作按攤銷成本計量          on specified dates to cash flows that are solely
   的金融資產。                      payments of principal and interest on the principal
                               amount outstanding and are held within a business
                               model with the objective to hold in order to collect
                               contractual cash flows. For such purpose, the time
                               deposits are accounted for as financial assets at
                               amortised cost.

   出一次性付款以向業主收購租賃土地，租          in its operations. Lump sum payments were made
   期為20年。樓宇的租期通常為2至26年，        upfront to acquire the leased land from the owners
   而汽車的租期通常介乎 1 至 3 年。其他設      with lease periods of 20 years. Leases of buildings
   備的租期一般為12 個月或以下及 ╱ 或個       generally have lease terms between 2 and 26 years,
   別價值較低。                      while motor vehicles generally have lease terms
                               between 1 and 3 years. Other equipment generally

                                                                     (continued)
                於各報告日期使用撥備矩陣進行減值分                                       An impairment analysis is performed at each
                析，以計量預期信貸虧損。撥備率乃基                                       reporting date using a provision matrix to measure
                於具有類似虧損模式的多個客戶分類組                                       ECLs. The provision rates are based on days past
                別（即客戶類型及服務類型）的逾期天數                                      due for groupings of various customer segments
                釐定。計算反映概率加權結果、貨幣時                                       with similar loss patterns (i.e. customer type
                間值及於報告日期有關過往事件、當前                                       and service type). The calculation reflects the
                狀況及未來經濟狀況預測的合理及有支                                       probability-weighted outcome, the time value of

         31. 儲備                                                  31. RESERVES
                本集團於本年度及先前年度的儲備款額及                                  The amounts of the Group’s reserves and the

                列。                                                  are presented in the consolidated statement of
                                                                    changes in equity of the financial statements.


                (a)    合併儲備                                         (a)   Merger reserve
                       本集團的合併儲備指本集團現時旗                                    The merger reserve of the Group represents
                       下的附屬公司繳足股本總額與本集                                    the difference between the aggregate of the
                       團就共同控制下的業務合併支付的                                    paid-up share capital of the subsidiaries now
                       代價之間的差額。                                           comprising the Group and the consideration
                                                                          paid by the Group for the business
                                                                          combination under common control.

                (b)    法定盈餘儲備                                       (b)   Statutory surplus reserve
                       根據中國公司法及集團公司各自之                                    In accordance with the Company Law of
                       組織章程，於中國註冊的各公司                                     the PRC and the respective articles of
                       須劃撥 10% 根據中國公認會計準                                  association of the group companies, each of
                       則釐定的除稅後溢利至法定盈餘儲                                    the companies that is domiciled in the PRC
                       備，直至達到註冊資本的 50% 為                                  is required to allocate 10% of its profit after
                       止。轉撥至該儲備須於向股東作出                                    tax, as determined in accordance with PRC
                       股息分派前作出。                                           GAAP, to the statutory surplus reserve until
                                                                          the reserve reaches 50% of the registered
                                                                          capital. The transfer to this reserve must be
                                                                          made before the distribution of a dividend to
                                                                          shareholders.


                       法定盈餘儲備為不可分配，除清盤                                    The statutory surplus reserve is non-
                       情況受有關中國法例所載若干限制                                    distributable except that in the event of
                       的規限，可用作抵銷累計虧損或資                                    liquidation where it can be used to offset
                       本化為繳足股本外。                                          accumulated losses or be capitalised as
                                                                          paid-up capital, subject to certain restrictions
                                                                          set out in the relevant PRC regulations.

  可進行。                      cash demands, subject to approval by management

  維持充裕的現金儲備及從主要金融機構取        covenants to ensure that it maintains sufficient

  較長期間的流動資金需求。              funding from major financial institutions to meet its

  本集團管理其資本，以確保本集團內實體                     The Group manages its capital to ensure that

  權益平衡為股東帶來最大回報。                         a going concern while maximising the return to

  總負債除以總資產），計及資本成本及與                     asset ratio, which is total liabilities divided by total
  各類資本相關的風險。本集團將透過籌集                     assets, on a continuous basis, taking into account
  新債務及贖回現有債務而平衡整體資本架                     the cost of capital and the risks associated with
  構，並管理負債與資產比率。本集團的整                     each class of capital. The Group will balance its
  體策略於年內維持不變。                            overall capital structure through the raising of new
                                         debts as well as the redemption of the existing
                                         debts, and manage the liability to asset ratio. The
                                         Group’s overall strategy remained unchanged
